title: "Letta API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Letta plugin in Corsair: every `letta.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Access Tokens

### create

`accessTokens.create`

Create token (Cloud-only). Create a new client side access token with the specified configuration. Mints a client-side access token (it expires after 5 minutes unless expires_at says otherwise).

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.accessTokens.create({
	hostname: 'example',
	policy: [{
		id: 'doc_8f2k1x9q',
		access: ['read_messages'],
		type: 'agent',
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `hostname` | `string` | Yes | The hostname of the client side application. Please specify the full URL including the protocol (http or https). |
| `policy` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `expires_at` | `string` | No | The expiration date of the token. If not provided, the token will expire in 5 minutes |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="policy full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  access: (read_messages | write_messages | read_agent | write_agent)[],
  type: agent
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `token` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hostname` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `policy` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="policy full type">

```ts
{
  data?: {
    id?: string | null,
    access?: string[] | null,
    type?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  version?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### delete

`accessTokens.delete`

Delete token (Cloud-only). Delete a client side access token. Letta Cloud refused every delete on 11-27 (400, its own body check fails even with no body); tokens expire on their own. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.accessTokens.delete({ token: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `token` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `any` | No | — |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### list

`accessTokens.list`

List tokens (Cloud-only). List all client side access tokens for the current account. This is only available for cloud users.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.accessTokens.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentId` | `string` | No | The agent ID to filter tokens by. If provided, only tokens for this agent will be returned. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | The number of tokens to return per page. Defaults to 10. |
| `offset` | `number` | No | The offset for pagination. Defaults to 0. |



**Output:** `object[] \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  hasNextPage?: boolean | null,
  tokens?: {
    token?: string | null,
    expiresAt?: string | null,
    hostname?: string | null,
    policy?: {
      data?: {
        id?: string | null,
        access?: string[] | null,
        type?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      version?: string | null
    } | null
  }[] | null
}[] | {
  hasNextPage?: boolean | null,
  tokens?: {
    token?: string | null,
    expiresAt?: string | null,
    hostname?: string | null,
    policy?: {
      data?: {
        id?: string | null,
        access?: string[] | null,
        type?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      version?: string | null
    } | null
  }[] | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Agents

### archives.attach

`agents.archives.attach`

Attach Archive To Agent. Attach an archive to an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.archives.attach({ agent_id: 'example', archive_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `archive_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### archives.detach

`agents.archives.detach`

Detach Archive From Agent. Detach an archive from an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.archives.detach({ agent_id: 'example', archive_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `archive_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### blocks.attach

`agents.blocks.attach`

Attach Block To Agent. Attach a core memory block to an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.blocks.attach({ agent_id: 'example', block_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `block_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the block in the format 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

---

### blocks.detach

`agents.blocks.detach`

Detach Block From Agent. Detach a core memory block from an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.blocks.detach({ agent_id: 'example', block_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `block_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the block in the format 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### blocks.get

`agents.blocks.get`

Retrieve Block For Agent. Retrieve a core memory block from an agent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.blocks.get({ agent_id: 'example', block_label: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `block_label` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the block. |
| `value` | `string \| null` | No | Value of the block. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The base template id of the block. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this Block. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | Description of the block. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | (Deprecated) If set to True, the block will be hidden. |
| `is_template` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the block is a template (e.g. saved human/persona options). |
| `label` | `string \| null` | No | Label of the block (e.g. 'human', 'persona') in the context window. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that last updated this Block. |
| `limit` | `number \| null` | No | Character limit of the block. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Metadata of the block. |
| `preserve_on_migration` | `boolean \| null` | No | (Deprecated) Preserve the block on template migration. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The associated project id. |
| `read_only` | `boolean \| null` | No | (Deprecated) Whether the agent has read-only access to the block. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the block. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The id of the template. |
| `template_name` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The name of the block template (if it is a template). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="metadata full type">

```ts
{
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### blocks.list

`agents.blocks.list`

List Blocks For Agent. Retrieve the core memory blocks of a specific agent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.blocks.list({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (block ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (block ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of blocks to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for blocks by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  value?: string | null,
  base_template_id?: string | null,
  created_by_id?: string | null,
  deployment_id?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  entity_id?: string | null,
  hidden?: boolean | null,
  is_template?: boolean | null,
  label?: string | null,
  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
  limit?: number | null,
  metadata?: {
  } | null,
  preserve_on_migration?: boolean | null,
  project_id?: string | null,
  read_only?: boolean | null,
  tags?: string[] | null,
  template_id?: string | null,
  template_name?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### blocks.update

`agents.blocks.update`

Update Block For Agent. Updates a core memory block of an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.blocks.update({ agent_id: 'example', block_label: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `block_label` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `base_template_id` | `string` | No | The base template id of the block. |
| `deployment_id` | `string` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Description of the block. |
| `entity_id` | `string` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, the block will be hidden. |
| `is_template` | `boolean` | No | Whether the block is a template (e.g. saved human/persona options). |
| `label` | `string` | No | Label of the block (e.g. 'human', 'persona') in the context window. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Character limit of the block. |
| `metadata` | `object` | No | Metadata of the block. |
| `preserve_on_migration` | `boolean` | No | Preserve the block on template migration. |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | The associated project id. |
| `read_only` | `boolean` | No | Whether the agent has read-only access to the block. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | The tags to associate with the block. |
| `template_id` | `string` | No | The id of the template. |
| `template_name` | `string` | No | Name of the block if it is a template. |
| `value` | `string` | No | Value of the block. |

| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The id of the template. |
| `template_name` | `string \| null` | No | (Deprecated) The name of the block template (if it is a template). |

---

### create

`agents.create`

Create Agent. Create an agent. (POST /v1/agents/)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.create({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_type` | `memgpt_agent \| memgpt_v2_agent \| letta_v1_agent \| react_agent \| workflow_agent \| split_thread_agent \| sleeptime_agent \| voice_convo_agent \| voice_sleeptime_agent` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. The base template id of the agent. |
| `block_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the blocks used by the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `context_window_limit` | `number` | No | The context window limit used by the agent. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_chunk_size` | `number` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. The embedding chunk size used by the agent. |
| `embedding_config` | `object` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_reasoner` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure reasoning instead. Whether to enable internal extended thinking step for a reasoner model. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `folder_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the folders used by the agent. |
| `from_template` | `string` | No | Deprecated: please use the 'create agents from a template' endpoint instead. |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `include_base_tool_rules` | `boolean` | No | If true, attaches the Letta base tool rules (e.g. deny all tools not explicitly allowed). |
| `include_base_tools` | `boolean` | No | If true, attaches the Letta core tools (e.g. core_memory related functions). |
| `include_default_source` | `boolean` | No | If true, automatically creates and attaches a default data source for this agent. |
| `initial_message_sequence` | `object[]` | No | The initial set of messages to put in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `llm_config` | `object` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `max_files_open` | `number` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `max_reasoning_tokens` | `number` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure reasoning tokens instead. The maximum number of tokens to generate for reasoning step. |
| `max_tokens` | `number` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure max output tokens instead. The maximum number of tokens to generate, including reasoning step. |
| `memory_blocks` | `object[]` | No | The blocks to create in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `memory_variables` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Deprecated: Only relevant for creating agents from a template. Use the 'create agents from a template' endpoint instead. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `metadata` | `object` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string` | No | The model handle for the agent to use (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object` | No | The model settings for the agent. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `parallel_tool_calls` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: Use `model_settings` to configure parallel tool calls instead. If set to True, enables parallel tool calling. |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project` | `string` | No | Deprecated: Project should now be passed via the X-Project header instead of in the request body. If using the SDK, this can be done via the x_project parameter. |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `reasoning` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure reasoning instead. Whether to enable reasoning for this agent. |
| `response_format` | `object` | No | Deprecated: Use `model_settings` field to configure response format instead. The response format for the agent. |
| `secrets` | `Record<string, string>` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `source_ids` | `string[]` | No | Deprecated: Use `folder_ids` field instead. The ids of the sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `template` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. |
| `template_id` | `string` | No | Deprecated: No longer used. The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Deprecated: Use `secrets` field instead. Environment variables for tool execution. |
| `tool_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the tools used by the agent. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[]` | No | The tool rules governing the agent. |
| `tools` | `string[]` | No | The tools used by the agent. |

Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |

| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### delete

`agents.delete`

Delete Agent. Delete an agent. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.delete({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### exportFile

`agents.exportFile`

Export Agent. Export the serialized JSON representation of an agent, formatted with indentation. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.exportFile({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | No | Conversation ID to export. If provided, uses messages from this conversation instead of the agent's global message history. |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | — |
| `scrub_messages` | `boolean` | No | If True, excludes all messages from the export. Useful for sharing agent configs without conversation history. |
| `use_legacy_format` | `boolean` | No | If True, exports using the legacy single-agent 'v1' format with inline tools/blocks. If False, exports using the new multi-entity 'v2' format, with separate agents, tools, blocks, files, etc. |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### files.close

`agents.files.close`

Close File For Agent. Closes a specific file for a given agent. This endpoint marks a specific file as closed in the agent's file state. The file will be removed from the agent's working memory view. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.files.close({ agent_id: 'example', file_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `file_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the file in the format 'file-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### files.closeAll

`agents.files.closeAll`

Close All Files For Agent. Closes all currently open files for a given agent. This endpoint updates the file state for the agent so that no files are marked as open. Typically used to reset the working memory view for the agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.files.closeAll({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### files.list

`agents.files.list`

List Files For Agent. Get the files attached to an agent with their open/closed status. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.files.list({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (file ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'file-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (file ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'file-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Pagination cursor from previous response (deprecated, use before/after) |
| `is_open` | `boolean` | No | Filter by open status (true for open files, false for closed files) |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of files to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for files by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `files` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `has_more` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="files full type">

```ts
{
  files?: {
    id?: string | null,

    end_line?: number | null,
    last_accessed_at?: string | null,

    visible_content?: string | null
  }[] | null,

  next_cursor?: string | null
}[] | null
```

</Accordion>

### files.open

`agents.files.open`

Open File For Agent. Opens a specific file for a given agent. This endpoint marks a specific file as open in the agent's file state. The file will be included in the agent's working memory view. Returns a list of file names that were closed due to LRU eviction. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.files.open({ agent_id: 'example', file_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `file_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the file in the format 'file-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### folders.attach

`agents.folders.attach`

Attach Folder To Agent. Attach a folder to an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.folders.attach({ agent_id: 'example', folder_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `folder_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the source in the format 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

---

### folders.detach

`agents.folders.detach`

Detach Folder From Agent. Detach a folder from an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.folders.detach({ agent_id: 'example', folder_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `folder_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the source in the format 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### folders.list

`agents.folders.list`

List Folders For Agent. Get the folders associated with an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.folders.list({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (source ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (source ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of sources to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for sources by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>

```ts

  id?: string | null,
  embedding_config?: {
    embedding_dim?: number | null,

  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
  metadata?: {
  } | null,

}[]
```

### get

`agents.get`

Retrieve Agent. Get the state of the agent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.get({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `include` | `(agent.blocks \| agent.identities \| agent.managed_group \| agent.pending_approval \| agent.secrets \| agent.sources \| agent.tags \| agent.tools)[]` | No | Specify which relational fields to include in the response. No relationships are included by default. |
| `include_relationships` | `string[]` | No | Specify which relational fields (e.g., 'tools', 'sources', 'memory') to include in the response. If not provided, all relationships are loaded by default. Using this can optimize performance by reducing unnecessary joins.This is a legacy parameter, and no longer supported after 1.0.0 SDK versions. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |

### identities.attach

`agents.identities.attach`

Attach Identity To Agent. Attach an identity to an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.identities.attach({ agent_id: 'example', identity_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### identities.detach

`agents.identities.detach`

Detach Identity From Agent. Detach an identity from an agent. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.identities.detach({ agent_id: 'example', identity_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### importFile

`agents.importFile`

Import Agent. Import a serialized agent file and recreate the agent(s) in the system. Returns the IDs of all imported agents. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.importFile({
	file: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `override_embedding_model` | `string` | No | — |
| `file` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The .af agent file to import. |
| `model` | `string` | No | Model handle to use instead of the file's default model. |
| `embedding` | `string` | No | Embedding handle to use instead of the file’s default. |
| `override_existing_tools` | `boolean` | No | Replace tools that already exist with the same name. |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | The project to import the agent into. |
| `strip_messages` | `boolean` | No | Import the agent without its message history. |
| `secrets` | `string` | No | Secrets as a JSON string, used by tools when they run. |
| `name` | `string` | No | A name for the imported agent. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="file full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | List of IDs of the imported agents |



---

### list

`agents.list`

List Agents. Get a list of all agents. (GET /v1/agents/)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination |
| `ascending` | `boolean` | No | Whether to sort agents oldest to newest (True) or newest to oldest (False, default) |
| `base_template_id` | `string` | No | Search agents by base template ID |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination |
| `created_by_id` | `string` | No | Filter agents by the user who created them. |
| `identifier_keys` | `string[]` | No | Search agents by identifier keys |
| `identity_id` | `string` | No | Search agents by identity ID |
| `include` | `(agent.blocks \| agent.identities \| agent.managed_group \| agent.pending_approval \| agent.secrets \| agent.sources \| agent.tags \| agent.tools)[]` | No | Specify which relational fields to include in the response. No relationships are included by default. |
| `include_relationships` | `string[]` | No | Specify which relational fields (e.g., 'tools', 'sources', 'memory') to include in the response. If not provided, all relationships are loaded by default. Using this can optimize performance by reducing unnecessary joins.This is a legacy parameter, and no longer supported after 1.0.0 SDK versions. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `end_turn \| error \| llm_api_error \| invalid_llm_response \| invalid_tool_call \| max_steps \| max_tokens_exceeded \| no_tool_call \| tool_rule \| cancelled \| insufficient_credits \| requires_approval \| context_window_overflow_in_system_prompt` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Limit for pagination |
| `match_all_tags` | `boolean` | No | If True, only returns agents that match ALL given tags. Otherwise, return agents that have ANY of the passed-in tags. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name of the agent |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for agents by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| updated_at \| last_run_completion` | No | Field to sort by |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | Search agents by project ID - this will default to your default project on cloud |
| `query_text` | `string` | No | Search agents by name |
| `sort_by` | `string` | No | Field to sort by. Options: 'created_at' (default), 'last_run_completion' |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | List of tags to filter agents by |
| `template_id` | `string` | No | Search agents by template ID |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

### messages.cancel

`agents.messages.cancel`

Cancel Message. Cancel runs associated with an agent. If run_ids are passed in, cancel those in particular. Note to cancel active runs associated with an agent, redis is required.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.cancel({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `run_ids` | `string[]` | No | Optional list of run IDs to cancel |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### messages.compact

`agents.messages.compact`

Summarize Messages. Summarize an agent's conversation history.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.compact({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `compaction_settings` | `object` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |

### messages.create

`agents.messages.create`

Create Message. Process a user message and return the agent's response. This endpoint accepts a message from a user and processes it through the agent. Note: Sending multiple concurrent requests to the same agent can lead to undefined behavior. Runs the agent: it calls its model (spending tokens) and may run tools.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.create({ agent_id: 'agent-3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', input: 'What do you remember about me?' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `assistant_message_tool_kwarg` | `string` | No | The name of the message argument in the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `assistant_message_tool_name` | `string` | No | The name of the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `background` | `boolean` | No | Whether to process the request in the background (only used when streaming=true). |
| `client_skills` | `object[]` | No | Client-side skills available in the environment. These are rendered in the system prompt's available skills section alongside agent-scoped skills from MemFS. |
| `client_tools` | `object[]` | No | Client-side tools that the agent can call. When the agent calls a client-side tool, execution pauses and returns control to the client to execute the tool and provide the result via a ToolReturn. |
| `enable_thinking` | `string` | No | If set to True, enables reasoning before responses or tool calls from the agent. |
| `include_compaction_messages` | `boolean` | No | If True, compaction events emit structured `SummaryMessage` and `EventMessage` types. If False (default), compaction messages are not included in the response. |
| `include_pings` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include periodic keepalive ping messages in the stream to prevent connection timeouts (only used when streaming=true). |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Only return specified message types in the response. If `None` (default) returns all messages. |
| `input` | `string \| object[] \| object` | No | Syntactic sugar for a single user message. Equivalent to messages=[&#123;'role': 'user', 'content': input&#125;]. |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | Maximum number of steps the agent should take to process the request. |
| `messages` | `object[]` | No | The messages to be sent to the agent. |
| `override_model` | `string` | No | Model handle to use for this request instead of the agent's default model. This allows sending a message to a different model without changing the agent's configuration. |
| `override_system` | `string` | No | Optional per-request system prompt override. When set, this is passed directly to the underlying LLM request and bypasses the persisted/compiled system message for that request. |
| `return_logprobs` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns log probabilities of the output tokens in the response. Useful for RL training. Only supported for OpenAI-compatible providers (including SGLang). |
| `return_token_ids` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns token IDs and logprobs for ALL LLM generations in the agent step, not just the last one. Uses SGLang native /generate endpoint. Returns 'turns' field with TurnTokenData for each assistant/tool turn. Required for proper multi-turn RL training with loss masking. |
| `stream_tokens` | `boolean` | No | Flag to determine if individual tokens should be streamed, rather than streaming per step (only used when streaming=true). |
| `streaming` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns a streaming response (Server-Sent Events). If False (default), returns a complete response. |
| `top_logprobs` | `number` | No | Number of most likely tokens to return at each position (0-20). Requires return_logprobs=True. |
| `use_assistant_message` | `boolean` | No | Whether the server should parse specific tool call arguments (default `send_message`) as `AssistantMessage` objects. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="client_skills full type">

```ts
{
  description: string,
  location: string,
  name: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="client_tools full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  description?: string,
  parameters?: {
  }
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
string | (
  {
    text: string,
    signature?: string,
    type?: text
  } | {
    source: {
      url: string,
      type?: url
    } | {
      data: string,
      media_type: string,
      detail?: string,
      type?: base64
    } | {
      file_id: string,
      data?: string,
      detail?: string,
      media_type?: string,
      type?: letta
    },
    type?: image
  } | {
    id: string,
    input: {
    },
    name: string,
    signature?: string,
    type?: tool_call
```

| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The usage statistics of the agent. |
| `logprobs` | `object \| null` | No | Log probabilities of the output tokens from the last LLM call. Only present if return_logprobs was enabled. |
| `turns` | `object[] \| null` | No | Token data for all LLM generations in multi-turn agent interaction. Includes token IDs and logprobs for each assistant turn, plus tool result content. Only present if return_token_ids was enabled. Used for RL training with loss masking. |

---

### messages.createAsync

`agents.messages.createAsync`

Create Message Async. Asynchronously process a user message and return a run object. The actual processing happens in the background, and the status can be checked using the run ID. This is "asynchronous" in the sense that it's a background run and explicitly must be fetched by the run ID. Runs the agent in the background and returns a run to poll with runs.get. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.createAsync({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `assistant_message_tool_kwarg` | `string` | No | The name of the message argument in the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `assistant_message_tool_name` | `string` | No | The name of the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `callback_url` | `string` | No | Optional callback URL to POST to when the job completes |
| `client_skills` | `object[]` | No | Client-side skills available in the environment. These are rendered in the system prompt's available skills section alongside agent-scoped skills from MemFS. |
| `client_tools` | `object[]` | No | Client-side tools that the agent can call. When the agent calls a client-side tool, execution pauses and returns control to the client to execute the tool and provide the result via a ToolReturn. |
| `enable_thinking` | `string` | No | If set to True, enables reasoning before responses or tool calls from the agent. |
| `include_compaction_messages` | `boolean` | No | If True, compaction events emit structured `SummaryMessage` and `EventMessage` types. If False (default), compaction messages are not included in the response. |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Only return specified message types in the response. If `None` (default) returns all messages. |
| `input` | `string \| object[] \| object` | No | Syntactic sugar for a single user message. Equivalent to messages=[&#123;'role': 'user', 'content': input&#125;]. |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | Maximum number of steps the agent should take to process the request. |
| `messages` | `object[]` | No | The messages to be sent to the agent. |
| `override_model` | `string` | No | Model handle to use for this request instead of the agent's default model. This allows sending a message to a different model without changing the agent's configuration. |
| `override_system` | `string` | No | Optional per-request system prompt override. When set, this is passed directly to the underlying LLM request and bypasses the persisted/compiled system message for that request. |
| `return_logprobs` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns log probabilities of the output tokens in the response. Useful for RL training. Only supported for OpenAI-compatible providers (including SGLang). |
| `return_token_ids` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns token IDs and logprobs for ALL LLM generations in the agent step, not just the last one. Uses SGLang native /generate endpoint. Returns 'turns' field with TurnTokenData for each assistant/tool turn. Required for proper multi-turn RL training with loss masking. |
| `top_logprobs` | `number` | No | Number of most likely tokens to return at each position (0-20). Requires return_logprobs=True. |
| `use_assistant_message` | `boolean` | No | Whether the server should parse specific tool call arguments (default `send_message`) as `AssistantMessage` objects. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |

**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Run |
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the agent associated with the run. |
| `background` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the run was created in background mode. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template ID that the run belongs to. |
| `callback_error` | `string \| null` | No | Optional error message from attempting to POST the callback endpoint. |
| `callback_sent_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the callback was last attempted. |
| `callback_status_code` | `number \| null` | No | HTTP status code returned by the callback endpoint. |
| `callback_url` | `string \| null` | No | If set, POST to this URL when the run completes. |
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the run was completed. |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the conversation associated with the run. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the run was created. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Additional metadata for the run. |
