### messages.list

`agents.messages.list`

List Messages. Retrieve message history for an agent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.list({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `assistant_message_tool_kwarg` | `string` | No | The name of the message argument. |
| `assistant_message_tool_name` | `string` | No | The name of the designated message tool. |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | No | Conversation ID to filter messages by. |
| `group_id` | `string` | No | Group ID to filter messages by. |
| `include_err` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include error messages and error statuses. For debugging purposes only. |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Message types to include in response. When null, all message types are returned. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of messages to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for messages by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `use_assistant_message` | `boolean` | No | Whether to use assistant messages |



**Output:** `object[]`

### messages.reset

`agents.messages.reset`

Reset Messages. Resets the messages for an agent. Clears the agent's message history.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.reset({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `add_default_initial_messages` | `boolean` | No | If true, adds the default initial messages after resetting. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

---

### messages.stream

`agents.messages.stream`

Create Message Streaming. Process a user message and return the agent's response. Deprecated: Use the POST /&#123;agent_id&#125;/messages endpoint with streaming=true in the request body instead. Note: Sending multiple concurrent requests to the same agent can lead to undefined behavior. Runs the agent like agents.messages.create and returns the server-sent events as text.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.messages.stream({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `assistant_message_tool_kwarg` | `string` | No | The name of the message argument in the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `assistant_message_tool_name` | `string` | No | The name of the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `background` | `boolean` | No | Whether to process the request in the background (only used when streaming=true). |
| `client_skills` | `object[]` | No | Client-side skills available in the environment. These are rendered in the system prompt's available skills section alongside agent-scoped skills from MemFS. |
| `client_tools` | `object[]` | No | Client-side tools that the agent can call. When the agent calls a client-side tool, execution pauses and returns control to the client to execute the tool and provide the result via a ToolReturn. |
| `enable_thinking` | `string` | No | If set to True, enables reasoning before responses or tool calls from the agent. |
| `include_compaction_messages` | `boolean` | No | If True, compaction events emit structured `SummaryMessage` and `EventMessage` types. If False (default), compaction messages are not included in the response. |
| `include_pings` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include periodic keepalive ping messages in the stream to prevent connection timeouts (only used when streaming=true). |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Only return specified message types in the response. If `None` (default) returns all messages. |
| `input` | `string \| object[] \| object` | No | Syntactic sugar for a single user message. Equivalent to messages=[&#123;'role': 'user', 'content': input&#125;]. |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | Maximum number of steps the agent should take to process the request. |
| `messages` | `object[]` | No | The messages to be sent to the agent. |
| `override_model` | `string` | No | Model handle to use for this request instead of the agent's default model. This allows sending a message to a different model without changing the agent's configuration. |
| `override_system` | `string` | No | Optional per-request system prompt override. When set, this is passed directly to the underlying LLM request and bypasses the persisted/compiled system message for that request. |
| `return_logprobs` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns log probabilities of the output tokens in the response. Useful for RL training. Only supported for OpenAI-compatible providers (including SGLang). |
| `return_token_ids` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns token IDs and logprobs for ALL LLM generations in the agent step, not just the last one. Uses SGLang native /generate endpoint. Returns 'turns' field with TurnTokenData for each assistant/tool turn. Required for proper multi-turn RL training with loss masking. |
| `stream_tokens` | `boolean` | No | Flag to determine if individual tokens should be streamed, rather than streaming per step (only used when streaming=true). |
| `streaming` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns a streaming response (Server-Sent Events). If False (default), returns a complete response. |
| `top_logprobs` | `number` | No | Number of most likely tokens to return at each position (0-20). Requires return_logprobs=True. |
| `use_assistant_message` | `boolean` | No | Whether the server should parse specific tool call arguments (default `send_message`) as `AssistantMessage` objects. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |

### passages.search

`agents.passages.search`

Search Archival Memory. Search archival memory using semantic (embedding-based) search with optional temporal filtering. This endpoint allows manual triggering of archival memory searches, enabling users to query an agent's archival memory store directly via the API.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.passages.search({ agent_id: 'example', query: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | String to search for using semantic similarity |
| `end_datetime` | `string` | No | Filter results to passages created before this datetime |
| `start_datetime` | `string` | No | Filter results to passages created after this datetime |
| `tag_match_mode` | `any \| all` | No | How to match tags - 'any' to match passages with any of the tags, 'all' to match only passages with all tags |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Optional list of tags to filter search results |
| `top_k` | `number` | No | Maximum number of results to return. Uses system default if not specified |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `count` | `number \| null` | No | Total number of results returned |
| `results` | `object[] \| null` | No | List of search results matching the query |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="results full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  content?: string | null,
  timestamp?: string | null,
  tags?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### recompile

`agents.recompile`

Recompile Agent. Manually trigger system prompt recompilation for an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.recompile({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `dry_run` | `boolean` | No | If True, do not persist changes; still returns the compiled system prompt. |
| `update_timestamp` | `boolean` | No | If True, update the in-context memory last edit timestamp embedded in the system prompt. |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### schedule.create

`agents.schedule.create`

Schedule Agent Message. Schedule a message to be sent by the agent at a specified time or on a recurring basis.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.schedule.create({
	agent_id: 'example',
	messages: [{ content: 'example', role: 'user' }],
	schedule: { scheduled_at: 1 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `messages` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

| `callback_url` | `string` | No | — |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message)[]` | No | — |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="messages full type">

```ts
{
  content: ((
    {
      text: string,
      signature?: string,
      type?: text
    } | {
      source: {
        data: string,
        media_type: string,
        detail?: string,
        type?: base64
      },
      type: image
    }
  )[] | string)[] | string,
  role: user | assistant | system,

  sender_id?: string,

}[]
```

```ts

}
```

### tools.attach

`agents.tools.attach`

Attach Tool To Agent. Attach a tool to an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.tools.attach({ agent_id: 'example', tool_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `tool_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the tool in the format 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

---

### tools.detach

`agents.tools.detach`

Detach Tool From Agent. Detach a tool from an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.tools.detach({ agent_id: 'example', tool_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `tool_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the tool in the format 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### tools.list

`agents.tools.list`

List Tools For Agent. Get tools from an existing agent.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.tools.list({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (tool ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (tool ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of tools to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for tools by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  args_json_schema?: {
  } | null,

  enable_parallel_execution?: boolean | null,

  } | null,
  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,

  } | null,

  npm_requirements?: {
    name?: string | null,
    version?: string | null
  }[] | null,

    name?: string | null,
    version?: string | null
  }[] | null,

  source_code?: string | null,
  source_type?: string | null,
  tags?: string[] | null,
  tool_type?: string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### tools.run

`agents.tools.run`

Run Tool For Agent. Trigger a tool by name on a specific agent, providing the necessary arguments. This endpoint executes a tool that is attached to the agent, using the agent's state and environment variables for execution context. Executes the tool with the arguments given; side effects are the tool’s own.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.tools.run({ agent_id: 'example', tool_name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `tool_name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `args` | `object` | No | Arguments to pass to the tool |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="args full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The status of the tool execution and return object |
| `agent_state` | `object \| null` | No | Representation of an agent's state. This is the state of the agent at a given time, and is persisted in the DB backend. The state has all the information needed to recreate a persisted agent. |
| `func_return` | `any \| null` | No | The function return object |
| `sandbox_config_fingerprint` | `string \| null` | No | The fingerprint of the config for the sandbox |
| `stderr` | `string[] \| null` | No | Captured stderr from the function invocation |
| `stdout` | `string[] \| null` | No | Captured stdout (prints, logs) from function invocation |

---

### tools.updateApproval

`agents.tools.updateApproval`

Update Approval For Tool. Modify the approval requirement for a tool attached to an agent. Accepts requires_approval via request body (preferred) or query parameter (deprecated). Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.tools.updateApproval({ agent_id: 'example', tool_name: 'example', requires_approval: true });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `tool_name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `requires_approval` | `boolean` | Yes | Whether the tool requires approval before execution |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

---

### update

`agents.update`

Update Agent. Update an existing agent. (PATCH /v1/agents/&#123;agent_id&#125;)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.agents.update({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the agent in the format 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `base_template_id` | `string` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `block_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the blocks used by the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `context_window_limit` | `number` | No | The context window limit used by the agent. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `folder_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the folders used by the agent. |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `end_turn \| error \| llm_api_error \| invalid_llm_response \| invalid_tool_call \| max_steps \| max_tokens_exceeded \| no_tool_call \| tool_rule \| cancelled \| insufficient_credits \| requires_approval \| context_window_overflow_in_system_prompt` | No | — |
| `llm_config` | `object` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `max_files_open` | `number` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `max_tokens` | `number` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure max output tokens instead. The maximum number of tokens to generate, including reasoning step. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object` | No | The model settings for the agent. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `parallel_tool_calls` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: Use `model_settings` to configure parallel tool calls instead. If set to True, enables parallel tool calling. |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `reasoning` | `boolean` | No | Deprecated: Use `model` field to configure reasoning instead. Whether to enable reasoning for this agent. |
| `response_format` | `object` | No | Deprecated: Use `model_settings` field to configure response format instead. The response format for the agent. |
| `secrets` | `Record<string, string>` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `source_ids` | `string[]` | No | Deprecated: Use `folder_ids` field instead. The ids of the sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `template_id` | `string` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead |
| `tool_ids` | `string[]` | No | The ids of the tools used by the agent. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[]` | No | The tool rules governing the agent. |

**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the agent. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_type` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Enum to represent the type of agent. |
| `blocks` | `object[] \| null` | No | The memory blocks used by the agent. |
| `llm_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for Language Model (LLM) connection and generation parameters. .. deprecated:: LLMConfig is deprecated and should not be used as an input or return type in API calls. Use the schemas in letta.schemas.model (ModelSettings, OpenAIModelSettings, etc.) instead. For conversion, use the _to_model() method or Model._from_llm_config() method. |
| `memory` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `blocks` field instead. The in-context memory of the agent. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the agent. |
| `sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folders` field instead. The sources used by the agent. |
| `system` | `string \| null` | No | The system prompt used by the agent. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | The tags associated with the agent. |
| `tools` | `object[] \| null` | No | The tools used by the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template id of the agent. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | The description of the agent. |
| `embedding` | `string \| null` | No | The embedding model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `enable_sleeptime` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, memory management will move to a background agent thread. |
| `entity_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will be hidden. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | The identities associated with this agent. |
| `identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `identities` field instead. The ids of the identities associated with this agent. |
| `last_run_completion` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the agent last completed a run. |
| `last_run_duration_ms` | `number \| null` | No | The duration in milliseconds of the agent's last run. |
| `last_stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `managed_group` | `object \| null` | No | The multi-agent group that this agent manages |
| `max_files_open` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum number of files that can be open at once for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `message_buffer_autoclear` | `boolean \| null` | No | If set to True, the agent will not remember previous messages (though the agent will still retain state via core memory blocks and archival/recall memory). Not recommended unless you have an advanced use case. |
| `message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The ids of the messages in the agent's in-context memory. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the agent. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle used by the agent (format: provider/model-name). |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings used by the agent. |
| `multi_agent_group` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `managed_group` field instead. The multi-agent group that this agent manages. |
| `pending_approval` | `object \| null` | No | A message representing a request for approval to call a tool (generated by the LLM to trigger tool execution). Args: id (str): The ID of the message date (datetime): The date the message was created in ISO format name (Optional[str]): The name of the sender of the message tool_call (ToolCall): The tool call |
| `per_file_view_window_char_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The per-file view window character limit for this agent. Setting this too high may exceed the context window, which will break the agent. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the project the agent belongs to. |
| `response_format` | `object \| null` | No | The response format used by the agent |
| `secrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | The environment variables for tool execution specific to this agent. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the template the agent belongs to. |
| `timezone` | `string \| null` | No | The timezone of the agent (IANA format). |
| `tool_exec_environment_variables` | `object[] \| null` | No | Deprecated: use `secrets` field instead. |
| `tool_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The list of tool rules. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

## Archives

### create

`archives.create`

Create Archive. Create a new archive. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.archives.create({ name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `description` | `string` | No | — |
| `embedding` | `string` | No | Embedding model handle for the archive |
| `embedding_config` | `object` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="embedding_config full type">

```ts
{
  embedding_dim: number,
  embedding_endpoint_type: openai | anthropic | bedrock | google_ai | google_vertex | azure | groq | ollama | webui | webui-legacy | lmstudio | lmstudio-legacy | llamacpp | koboldcpp | vllm | hugging-face | mistral | together | pinecone,
  embedding_model: string,
  azure_deployment?: string,
  azure_endpoint?: string,
  azure_version?: string,
  batch_size?: number,
  embedding_chunk_size?: number,
  embedding_endpoint?: string,
  handle?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Archive |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the archive |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the archive |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A description of the archive |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Additional metadata |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |
| `vector_db_provider` | `string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Supported vector database providers for archival memory |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="embedding_config full type">

```ts
{
  embedding_dim?: number | null,
  embedding_endpoint_type?: string | null,
  embedding_model?: string | null,
  azure_deployment?: string | null,
  azure_endpoint?: string | null,
  azure_version?: string | null,
  batch_size?: number | null,
  embedding_chunk_size?: number | null,
  embedding_endpoint?: string | null,

} | null
```

### delete

`archives.delete`

Delete Archive. Delete an archive by its ID. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.archives.delete({ archive_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archive_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the archive in the format 'archive-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

`archives.list`

List Archives. Get a list of all archives for the current organization with optional filters and pagination. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.archives.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (archive ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'archive-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Only archives attached to this agent ID |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (archive ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'archive-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of archives to return |
| `name` | `string` | No | Filter by archive name (exact match) |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for archives by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  created_by_id?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  embedding_config?: {
    embedding_dim?: number | null,
    embedding_endpoint_type?: string | null,
    embedding_model?: string | null,
    azure_deployment?: string | null,
    azure_endpoint?: string | null,
    azure_version?: string | null,
    batch_size?: number | null,
    embedding_chunk_size?: number | null,
    embedding_endpoint?: string | null,
    handle?: string | null
  } | null,
  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
  metadata?: {
  } | null,
  updated_at?: string | null,
  vector_db_provider?: string | string | string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### passages.create

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `embedding` | `number[] \| null` | No | The embedding of the passage. |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `text` | `string \| null` | No | The text of the passage. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Passage |
| `archive_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the archive containing this passage. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the passage. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `file_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the file associated with the passage. |
| `file_name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the file (only for source passages). |
| `is_deleted` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this passage is deleted or not. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | The metadata of the passage. |
| `source_id` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folder_id` field instead. The data source of the passage. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | Tags associated with this passage. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### passages.delete

`archives.passages.delete`

Delete Passage From Archive. Delete a passage from an archive. This permanently removes the passage from both the database and vector storage (if applicable). Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.archives.passages.delete({ archive_id: 'example', passage_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archive_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the archive in the format 'archive-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `passage_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the passage in the format 'passage-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### update

`archives.update`

Update Archive. Update an existing archive's name and/or description. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.archives.update({ archive_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archive_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the archive in the format 'archive-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `description` | `string` | No | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Archive |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the archive |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the archive |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A description of the archive |
| `embedding_config` | `object \| null` | No | Configuration for embedding model connection and processing parameters. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Additional metadata |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |
| `vector_db_provider` | `string \| string \| string \| null` | No | Supported vector database providers for archival memory |

## Blocks

### agents.list

`blocks.agents.list`

List Agents For Block. Retrieves all agents associated with the specified block. Raises a 404 if the block does not exist.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.blocks.agents.list({ block_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `block_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the block in the format 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (agent ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (agent ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `include` | `(agent.blocks \| agent.identities \| agent.managed_group \| agent.pending_approval \| agent.secrets \| agent.sources \| agent.tags \| agent.tools)[]` | No | Specify which relational fields to include in the response. No relationships are included by default. |
| `include_relationships` | `string[]` | No | Specify which relational fields (e.g., 'tools', 'sources', 'memory') to include in the response. If not provided, all relationships are loaded by default. Using this can optimize performance by reducing unnecessary joins.This is a legacy parameter, and no longer supported after 1.0.0 SDK versions. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of agents to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for agents by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`

### create

`blocks.create`

Create Block. (POST /v1/blocks/)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.blocks.create({ label: 'example', value: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `label` | `string` | Yes | Label of the block. |
| `value` | `string` | Yes | Value of the block. |
| `base_template_id` | `string` | No | The base template id of the block. |
| `deployment_id` | `string` | No | The id of the deployment. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Description of the block. |
| `entity_id` | `string` | No | The id of the entity within the template. |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | If set to True, the block will be hidden. |
| `is_template` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Character limit of the block. |
| `metadata` | `object` | No | Metadata of the block. |
| `preserve_on_migration` | `boolean` | No | Preserve the block on template migration. |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | The associated project id. |
| `read_only` | `boolean` | No | Whether the agent has read-only access to the block. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | The tags to associate with the block. |
| `template_id` | `string` | No | The id of the template. |
| `template_name` | `string` | No | Name of the block if it is a template. |

### list

`blocks.list`

List Blocks. (GET /v1/blocks/)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.blocks.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (block ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (block ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'block-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `connected_to_agents_count_eq` | `number[]` | No | Filter blocks by the exact number of connected agents. If provided, returns blocks that have exactly this number of connected agents. |
| `connected_to_agents_count_gt` | `number` | No | Filter blocks by the number of connected agents. If provided, returns blocks that have more than this number of connected agents. |
| `connected_to_agents_count_lt` | `number` | No | Filter blocks by the number of connected agents. If provided, returns blocks that have less than this number of connected agents. |
| `description_search` | `string` | No | Search blocks by description. If provided, returns blocks whose description matches the search query. This is a full-text search on block descriptions. |
| `identifier_keys` | `string[]` | No | Search agents by identifier keys |
| `identity_id` | `string` | No | The ID of the identity in the format 'identity-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `label` | `string` | No | Label to include (alphanumeric, hyphens, underscores, forward slashes) |
| `label_search` | `string` | No | Search blocks by label. If provided, returns blocks whose label matches the search query. This is a full-text search on block labels. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Number of blocks to return |
| `match_all_tags` | `boolean` | No | If True, only returns blocks that match ALL given tags. Otherwise, return blocks that have ANY of the passed-in tags. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name filter (alphanumeric, spaces, hyphens, underscores) |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for blocks by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | Search blocks by project id |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | List of tags to filter blocks by |
| `templates_only` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include only templates |
| `value_search` | `string` | No | Search blocks by value. If provided, returns blocks whose value matches the search query. This is a full-text search on block values. |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  value?: string | null,
  base_template_id?: string | null,
  created_by_id?: string | null,
  deployment_id?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  entity_id?: string | null,
  hidden?: boolean | null,
  is_template?: boolean | null,
  label?: string | null,
  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
  limit?: number | null,
  metadata?: {
  } | null,
  preserve_on_migration?: boolean | null,
  project_id?: string | null,
  read_only?: boolean | null,
  tags?: string[] | null,
  template_id?: string | null,
  template_name?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Conversations

### cancel

`conversations.cancel`

Cancel Conversation. Cancel runs associated with a conversation. Note: To cancel active runs, Redis is required. Agent-direct mode: Pass conversation_id="default" with agent_id query parameter to cancel runs for the agent's default conversation.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.cancel({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### create

`conversations.create`

Create Conversation. Create a new conversation for an agent.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.create({ agent_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The agent ID to create a conversation for |
| `context_window_limit` | `number` | No | The context window limit for this conversation (overrides agent's context window). |
| `description` | `string` | No | A generated description of the conversation used for search and bootstrap context. |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | Whether the new conversation should be hidden from listings. |
| `model` | `string` | No | The model handle for this conversation (overrides agent's model). Format: provider/model-name. |
| `model_settings` | `object` | No | The model settings for this conversation (overrides agent's model settings). |
| `summary` | `string` | No | A summary of the conversation. |

### delete

`conversations.delete`

Delete Conversation. Delete a conversation. The conversation will no longer appear in list operations. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.delete({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the conv in the format 'conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### fork

`conversations.fork`

Fork Conversation. Fork an existing conversation. Creates a new conversation that shares the same in-context messages as the source conversation, but with a newly compiled system message reflecting the latest memory block values. The forked conversation belongs to the same agent as the source.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.fork({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation |
| `hidden` | `boolean` | No | Whether the forked conversation should be hidden from listings |
| `message_id` | `string` | No | The ID of the message in the format 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the conversation. |
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the agent this conversation belongs to. |
| `archived` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the conversation is archived. |
| `archived_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp of when the conversation was archived. |
| `context_window_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The context window limit for this conversation (overrides agent's context window). |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A generated description of the conversation used for search and bootstrap context. |
| `in_context_message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The IDs of in-context messages for the conversation. Null means this field was not retrieved/hydrated for this response. |
| `last_message_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp of the most recent message request sent to this conversation. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle for this conversation (overrides agent's model). Format: provider/model-name. |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings for this conversation (overrides agent's model settings). |
| `summary` | `string \| null` | No | A summary of the conversation. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### list

`conversations.list`

List Conversations. List all conversations for an agent (or all conversations if agent_id not provided).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (conv ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | The agent ID to list conversations for (optional - returns all conversations if not provided) |
| `archive_status` | `unarchived \| archived \| all` | No | Whether to return unarchived conversations only, archived conversations only, or all conversations |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of conversations to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for conversations. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| last_run_completion \| last_message_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `summary_search` | `string` | No | Search for text within conversation summaries |



**Output:** `object[] \| object`

### messages.compact

`conversations.messages.compact`

Compact Conversation. Compact (summarize) a conversation's message history. This endpoint summarizes the in-context messages for a specific conversation, reducing the message count while preserving important context.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.messages.compact({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation. Use with conversation_id='default' in the URL path. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="compaction_settings full type">

### messages.create

`conversations.messages.create`

Send Conversation Message. Send a message to a conversation and get a response. This endpoint sends a message to an existing conversation. By default (streaming=true), returns a streaming response (Server-Sent Events). Set streaming=false to get a complete JSON response. Runs the agent in this conversation: it calls its model and may run tools. Letta streams by default here: the answer is the server-sent event text as a string unless you pass streaming: false.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.messages.create({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation. Use with conversation_id='default' in the URL path. |
| `assistant_message_tool_kwarg` | `string` | No | The name of the message argument in the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `assistant_message_tool_name` | `string` | No | The name of the designated message tool. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |
| `background` | `boolean` | No | Whether to process the request in the background (only used when streaming=true). |
| `client_skills` | `object[]` | No | Client-side skills available in the environment. These are rendered in the system prompt's available skills section alongside agent-scoped skills from MemFS. |
| `client_tools` | `object[]` | No | Client-side tools that the agent can call. When the agent calls a client-side tool, execution pauses and returns control to the client to execute the tool and provide the result via a ToolReturn. |
| `enable_thinking` | `string` | No | If set to True, enables reasoning before responses or tool calls from the agent. |
| `include_compaction_messages` | `boolean` | No | If True, compaction events emit structured `SummaryMessage` and `EventMessage` types. If False (default), compaction messages are not included in the response. |
| `include_pings` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include periodic keepalive ping messages in the stream to prevent connection timeouts (only used when streaming=true). |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Only return specified message types in the response. If `None` (default) returns all messages. |
| `input` | `string \| object[] \| object` | No | Syntactic sugar for a single user message. Equivalent to messages=[&#123;'role': 'user', 'content': input&#125;]. |
| `max_steps` | `number` | No | Maximum number of steps the agent should take to process the request. |
| `messages` | `object[]` | No | The messages to be sent to the agent. |
| `override_model` | `string` | No | Model handle to use for this request instead of the agent's default model. This allows sending a message to a different model without changing the agent's configuration. |
| `override_system` | `string` | No | Optional per-request system prompt override. When set, this is passed directly to the underlying LLM request and bypasses the persisted/compiled system message for that request. |
| `return_logprobs` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns log probabilities of the output tokens in the response. Useful for RL training. Only supported for OpenAI-compatible providers (including SGLang). |
| `return_token_ids` | `boolean` | No | If True, returns token IDs and logprobs for ALL LLM generations in the agent step, not just the last one. Uses SGLang native /generate endpoint. Returns 'turns' field with TurnTokenData for each assistant/tool turn. Required for proper multi-turn RL training with loss masking. |
| `stream_tokens` | `boolean` | No | Flag to determine if individual tokens should be streamed, rather than streaming per step (only used when streaming=true). |
| `streaming` | `boolean` | No | If True (default), returns a streaming response (Server-Sent Events). If False, returns a complete JSON response. |
| `top_logprobs` | `number` | No | Number of most likely tokens to return at each position (0-20). Requires return_logprobs=True. |
| `use_assistant_message` | `boolean` | No | Whether the server should parse specific tool call arguments (default `send_message`) as `AssistantMessage` objects. Still supported for legacy agent types, but deprecated for letta_v1_agent onward. |

### messages.list

`conversations.messages.list`

List Conversation Messages. List all messages in a conversation. Returns LettaMessage objects (UserMessage, AssistantMessage, etc.) for all messages in the conversation, with support for cursor-based pagination.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.messages.list({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `group_id` | `string` | No | Group ID to filter messages by. |
| `include_err` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include error messages and error statuses. For debugging purposes only. |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Message types to include in response. When null, all message types are returned. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of messages to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for messages by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `object[]`
