### messages.stream

`conversations.messages.stream`

Retrieve Conversation Stream. Resume the stream for the most recent active run in a conversation. This endpoint allows you to reconnect to an active background stream for a conversation, enabling recovery from network interruptions.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.messages.stream({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation. Use with conversation_id='default' in the URL path. |
| `batch_size` | `number` | No | Number of entries to read per batch. |
| `include_pings` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include periodic keepalive ping messages in the stream to prevent connection timeouts. |
| `otid` | `string` | No | Offline threading ID to look up the run_id. Bypasses active run lookup if run_id not provided. |
| `poll_interval` | `number` | No | Seconds to wait between polls when no new data. |
| `run_id` | `string` | No | Run ID to stream directly, bypassing run lookup. Use for recovery from duplicate requests. |
| `starting_after` | `number` | No | Sequence id to use as a cursor for pagination. Response will start streaming after this chunk sequence id |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `text` | `string` | Yes | The response body as text. |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | The content type Letta answered with. |



---

### recompile

`conversations.recompile`

Recompile Conversation. Manually trigger system prompt recompilation for a conversation.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.recompile({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The conversation identifier. Can be a conversation ID ('conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;'), 'default' for agent-direct mode (with agent_id parameter), or an agent ID ('agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;') for backwards compatibility (deprecated). |
| `dry_run` | `boolean` | No | If True, do not persist changes; still returns the compiled system prompt. |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation. Use with conversation_id='default' in the URL path. |
| `compaction_settings` | `object` | No | Configuration for conversation compaction / summarization. Per-model settings (temperature, max tokens, etc.) are derived from the default configuration for that handle. |

### update

`conversations.update`

Update Conversation. Update a conversation.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.conversations.update({ conversation_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the conv in the format 'conv-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `archived` | `boolean` | No | Whether the conversation is archived. |
| `context_window_limit` | `number` | No | The context window limit for this conversation (overrides agent's context window). |
| `description` | `string` | No | A generated description of the conversation used for search and bootstrap context. |
| `last_message_at` | `string` | No | Timestamp of the most recent message request sent to this conversation. |
| `model` | `string` | No | The model handle for this conversation (overrides agent's model). Format: provider/model-name. |
| `model_settings` | `object` | No | The model settings for this conversation (overrides agent's model settings). |
| `summary` | `string` | No | A summary of the conversation. |

| `archived_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp of when the conversation was archived. |
| `context_window_limit` | `number \| null` | No | The context window limit for this conversation (overrides agent's context window). |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was created. |
| `created_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A generated description of the conversation used for search and bootstrap context. |
| `in_context_message_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The IDs of in-context messages for the conversation. Null means this field was not retrieved/hydrated for this response. |
| `last_message_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp of the most recent message request sent to this conversation. |
| `last_updated_by_id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the user that made this object. |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The model handle for this conversation (overrides agent's model). Format: provider/model-name. |
| `model_settings` | `object \| null` | No | The model settings for this conversation (overrides agent's model settings). |
| `summary` | `string \| null` | No | A summary of the conversation. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the object was last updated. |

### sendMessage

`environments.sendMessage`

Send Message to Environment. Send a message to a specific environment connection.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.environments.sendMessage({
	connectionId: 'example',
	messages: [{ client_message_id: 'example', content: 'example', role: 'user' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connectionId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `messages` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `agentId` | `string` | No | — |
| `conversationId` | `string` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="messages full type">

```ts
(
  {
    client_message_id: string,
    content: string | {
      text: string,
      type: text
    }[],
    role: user,
    otid?: string
  } | {
    approvals: (
      {
        status: success | error,
        tool_call_id: string,
        tool_return: string | {
          text: string,
          type: text
        }[],
        stderr?: string[],
        stdout?: string[],
        type?: tool
      } | {
        approve: boolean,
        tool_call_id: string,
        reason?: string,
        type?: approval,
        updated_input?: {
        }
      }
    )[],
    type: approval
  }
)[]
```

</AccordionGroup>

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

## Folders

### agents.list

`folders.agents.list`

List Agents For Folder. Get all agent IDs that have the specified folder attached. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.folders.agents.list({ folder_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `folder_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the source in the format 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (agent ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (agent ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'agent-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of agents to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for agents by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### files.get

`folders.files.get`

Retrieve File. Retrieve a file from a folder by ID. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.folders.files.get({ folder_id: 'example', file_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `folder_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the source in the format 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `file_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the file in the format 'file-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `include_content` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include full file content |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the File |
| `source_id` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folder_id` field instead. The unique identifier of the source associated with the document. |
| `chunks_embedded` | `number \| null` | No | Number of chunks that have been embedded. |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | Optional full-text content of the file; only populated on demand due to its size. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the file. |
| `error_message` | `string \| null` | No | Optional error message if the file failed processing. |
| `file_creation_date` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the file. |
| `file_last_modified_date` | `string \| null` | No | The last modified date of the file. |
| `file_name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the file. |
| `file_path` | `string \| null` | No | The path to the file. |
| `file_size` | `number \| null` | No | The size of the file in bytes. |
| `file_type` | `string \| null` | No | The type of the file (MIME type). |
| `original_file_name` | `string \| null` | No | The original name of the file as uploaded. |
| `processing_status` | `string \| null` | No | The current processing status of the file (e.g. pending, parsing, embedding, completed, error). |
| `total_chunks` | `number \| null` | No | Total number of chunks for the file. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The update date of the file. |

### files.upload

`folders.files.upload`

Upload File To Folder. Upload a file to a data folder. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.folders.files.upload({
	folder_id: 'example',
	file: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `folder_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the source in the format 'source-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `duplicate_handling` | `skip \| error \| suffix \| replace` | No | How to handle duplicate filenames |
| `name` | `string` | No | Optional custom name to override the uploaded file's name |
| `file` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The file to upload. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="file full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the File |
| `source_id` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated: Use `folder_id` field instead. The unique identifier of the source associated with the document. |
| `chunks_embedded` | `number \| null` | No | Number of chunks that have been embedded. |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | Optional full-text content of the file; only populated on demand due to its size. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the file. |
| `error_message` | `string \| null` | No | Optional error message if the file failed processing. |
| `file_creation_date` | `string \| null` | No | The creation date of the file. |
| `file_last_modified_date` | `string \| null` | No | The last modified date of the file. |
| `file_name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the file. |
| `file_path` | `string \| null` | No | The path to the file. |
| `file_size` | `number \| null` | No | The size of the file in bytes. |
| `file_type` | `string \| null` | No | The type of the file (MIME type). |
| `original_file_name` | `string \| null` | No | The original name of the file as uploaded. |
| `processing_status` | `string \| null` | No | The current processing status of the file (e.g. pending, parsing, embedding, completed, error). |
| `total_chunks` | `number \| null` | No | Total number of chunks for the file. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The update date of the file. |

## Mcp Servers

### create

`mcpServers.create`

Create Mcp Server. Add a new MCP server to the Letta MCP server config.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.mcpServers.create({
	config: {
		args: ['example'],
		command: 'example',
	},
	server_name: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `config` | `object` | Yes | The MCP server configuration (Stdio, SSE, or Streamable HTTP) |
| `server_name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the MCP server |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts
{
  args: string[],
  command: string,
  env?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: stdio
} | {
  server_url: string,
  auth_header?: string,
  auth_token?: string,
  custom_headers?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: sse
} | {
  server_url: string,
  auth_header?: string,
  auth_token?: string,
  custom_headers?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: streamable_http
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `args` | `string[] \| null` | No | The arguments to pass to the command |
| `command` | `string \| null` | No | The command to run (MCP 'local' client will run this command) |
| `server_name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the MCP server |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Mcp_server |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables to set |
| `mcp_server_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### tools.run

`mcpServers.tools.run`

Run Mcp Tool. Execute a specific MCP tool The request body should contain the tool arguments in the ToolExecuteRequest format. Executes the MCP tool with the arguments given.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.mcpServers.tools.run({ mcp_server_id: 'example', tool_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `mcp_server_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `tool_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `args` | `object` | No | Arguments to pass to the tool |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="args full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `agent_state` | `object \| null` | No | Representation of an agent's state. This is the state of the agent at a given time, and is persisted in the DB backend. The state has all the information needed to recreate a persisted agent. |

---

### update

`mcpServers.update`

Update Mcp Server. Update an existing MCP server configuration.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.mcpServers.update({
	mcp_server_id: 'example',
	config: {
		args: ['example'],
		command: 'example',
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `mcp_server_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `config` | `object` | Yes | The MCP server configuration updates (Stdio, SSE, or Streamable HTTP) |
| `server_name` | `string` | No | The name of the MCP server |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="config full type">

```ts
{
  args: string[],
  command: string,
  env?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: stdio
} | {
  server_url: string,
  auth_header?: string,
  auth_token?: string,
  custom_headers?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: sse
} | {
  server_url: string,
  auth_header?: string,
  auth_token?: string,
  custom_headers?: Record<string, string>,
  mcp_server_type?: streamable_http
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `args` | `string[] \| null` | No | The arguments to pass to the command |
| `command` | `string \| null` | No | The command to run (MCP 'local' client will run this command) |
| `server_name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the MCP server |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Mcp_server |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables to set |
| `mcp_server_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

## Messages

### get

`messages.get`

Retrieve Message. Retrieve a message by ID.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.messages.get({ message_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the message in the format 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts

    id?: string | null,
```

### list

`messages.list`

List All Messages. List messages across all agents for the current user.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.messages.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (message ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'message-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | No | Conversation ID to filter messages by |
| `include_return_message_types` | `(system_message \| user_message \| assistant_message \| reasoning_message \| hidden_reasoning_message \| tool_call_message \| tool_return_message \| approval_request_message \| approval_response_message \| summary_message \| event_message)[]` | No | Message types to include in response. When null, all message types are returned. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of messages to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for messages by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |

### search

`messages.search`

Search All Messages. Search messages across the organization with optional agent filtering. Returns messages with FTS/vector ranks and total RRF score. This is a cloud-only feature.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.messages.search({ query: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `string` | Yes | Text query for full-text search |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Filter messages by agent ID |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | No | Filter messages by conversation ID |
| `end_date` | `string` | No | Filter messages created on or before this date |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of results to return |
| `search_mode` | `vector \| fts \| hybrid` | No | Search mode to use |
| `start_date` | `string` | No | Filter messages created after this date |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Models

### embeddings.list

`models.embeddings.list`

List Embedding Models. List available embedding models using the asynchronous implementation for improved performance. Returns EmbeddingModel format which extends EmbeddingConfig with additional metadata fields. Legacy EmbeddingConfig fields are marked as deprecated but still available for backward compatibility.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.models.embeddings.list({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output:** `object[] \| object`

## Passages

### search

`passages.search`

Search Passages. Search passages across the organization with optional agent and archive filtering. Returns passages with relevance scores. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.passages.search({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Filter passages by agent ID |
| `archive_id` | `string` | No | Filter passages by archive ID |
| `end_date` | `string` | No | Filter results to passages created before this datetime |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of results to return |
| `query` | `string` | No | Text query for semantic search |
| `start_date` | `string` | No | Filter results to passages created after this datetime |
| `tag_match_mode` | `any \| all` | No | How to match tags - 'any' to match passages with any of the tags, 'all' to match only passages with all tags |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Optional list of tags to filter search results |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `passage` | `object \| null` | No | Representation of a passage, which is stored in archival memory. |
| `score` | `number \| null` | No | Relevance score |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Additional metadata about the search result |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="passage full type">

```ts
{
  embedding?: number[] | null,
  embedding_config?: {
    embedding_dim?: number | null,
    embedding_endpoint_type?: string | null,

  file_name?: string | null,
  is_deleted?: boolean | null,
  last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
  metadata?: {
  } | null,
  source_id?: string | null,
  tags?: string[] | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
} | null
```

## Runs

### get

`runs.get`

Retrieve Run. Get the status of a run. (GET /v1/runs/&#123;run_id&#125;)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.runs.get({ run_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `run_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The human-friendly ID of the Run |
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the agent associated with the run. |
| `background` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the run was created in background mode. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template ID that the run belongs to. |
| `callback_error` | `string \| null` | No | Optional error message from attempting to POST the callback endpoint. |
| `callback_sent_at` | `string \| null` | No | Timestamp when the callback was last attempted. |
| `callback_status_code` | `number \| null` | No | HTTP status code returned by the callback endpoint. |
| `callback_url` | `string \| null` | No | If set, POST to this URL when the run completes. |
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the run was completed. |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the conversation associated with the run. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The timestamp when the run was created. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Additional metadata for the run. |
| `request_config` | `object \| null` | No | The request configuration for the run. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The current status of the run. |
| `stop_reason` | `string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `total_duration_ns` | `number \| null` | No | Total run duration in nanoseconds |
| `ttft_ns` | `number \| null` | No | Time to first token for a run in nanoseconds |

```ts
{
} | null
```

### list

`runs.list`

List Runs. List all runs. (GET /v1/runs/)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.runs.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `active` | `boolean` | No | Filter for active runs. |
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (run ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'run-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | The unique identifier of the agent associated with the run. |
| `agent_ids` | `string[]` | No | The unique identifiers of the agents associated with the run. Deprecated in favor of agent_id field. |
| `ascending` | `boolean` | No | Whether to sort agents oldest to newest (True) or newest to oldest (False, default). Deprecated in favor of order field. |
| `background` | `boolean` | No | If True, filters for runs that were created in background mode. |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (run ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'run-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | No | Filter runs by conversation ID. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of runs to return |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for runs by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `statuses` | `string[]` | No | Filter runs by status. Can specify multiple statuses. |
| `stop_reason` | `end_turn \| error \| llm_api_error \| invalid_llm_response \| invalid_tool_call \| max_steps \| max_tokens_exceeded \| no_tool_call \| tool_rule \| cancelled \| insufficient_credits \| requires_approval \| context_window_overflow_in_system_prompt` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

### messages.stream

`runs.messages.stream`

Retrieve Stream For Run. Reattaches to a background run’s stream; returns the server-sent events as text.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.runs.messages.stream({ run_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `run_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Agent ID for agent-direct mode with 'default' conversation. Use with conversation_id='default' in the URL path. |
| `batch_size` | `number` | No | Number of entries to read per batch. |
| `include_pings` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include periodic keepalive ping messages in the stream to prevent connection timeouts. |
| `otid` | `string` | No | Offline threading ID to look up the run_id. Bypasses active run lookup if run_id not provided. |
| `poll_interval` | `number` | No | Seconds to wait between polls when no new data. |
| `starting_after` | `number` | No | Sequence id to use as a cursor for pagination. Response will start streaming after this chunk sequence id |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `text` | `string` | Yes | The response body as text. |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | The content type Letta answered with. |

### trace.get

`runs.trace.get`

Retrieve Trace For Run. Retrieve OTEL trace spans for a run. Returns a filtered set of spans relevant for observability: - agent_step: Individual agent reasoning steps - tool executions: Tool call spans - Root span: The top-level request span - time_to_first_token: TTFT measurement span Requires ClickHouse to be… Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts

```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `run_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of spans to return |



**Output:** `any \| object`

```ts
  success: true
}
```

## Steps

### feedback.create

`steps.feedback.create`

Modify Feedback For Step. Modify feedback for a given step. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.steps.feedback.create({ step_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `step_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the step in the format 'step-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `feedback` | `positive \| negative` | No | Whether this feedback is positive or negative |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Feedback tags to add to the step |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the step. Assigned by the database. |
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the agent that performed the step. |

### list

`steps.list`

List Steps. List steps with optional pagination and date filters. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.steps.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (step ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'step-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Filter by the ID of the agent that performed the step |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (step ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'step-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `end_date` | `string` | No | Return steps before this ISO datetime (e.g. "2025-01-29T15:01:19-08:00") |
| `feedback` | `positive \| negative` | No | Filter by feedback |
| `has_feedback` | `boolean` | No | Filter by whether steps have feedback (true) or not (false) |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of steps to return |
| `model` | `string` | No | Filter by the name of the model used for the step |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for steps by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `project_id` | `string` | No | Filter by the project ID that is associated with the step (cloud only). |
| `start_date` | `string` | No | Return steps after this ISO datetime (e.g. "2025-01-29T15:01:19-08:00") |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Filter by tags |
| `trace_ids` | `string[]` | No | Filter by trace ids returned by the server |



**Output:** `object[]`

### metrics.get

`steps.metrics.get`

Retrieve Metrics For Step. Get step metrics by step ID. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.steps.metrics.get({ step_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `step_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the step in the format 'step-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The id of the step this metric belongs to (matches steps.id). |
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the agent. |
| `base_template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The base template ID that the step belongs to (cloud only). |
| `llm_request_ns` | `number \| null` | No | Time spent on LLM requests in nanoseconds. |
| `llm_request_start_ns` | `number \| null` | No | The timestamp of the start of the llm request in nanoseconds. |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | The project that the step belongs to (cloud only). |
| `provider_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the provider. |
| `run_id` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the run. |
| `step_ns` | `number \| null` | No | Total time for the step in nanoseconds. |
| `step_start_ns` | `number \| null` | No | The timestamp of the start of the step in nanoseconds. |
| `template_id` | `string \| null` | No | The template ID that the step belongs to (cloud only). |
| `tool_execution_ns` | `number \| null` | No | Time spent on tool execution in nanoseconds. |

## Tags

### list

`tags.list`

List Tags. Get the list of all tags (from agents and blocks) that have been created.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.tags.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Tag cursor for pagination. Returns tags that come after this tag in the specified sort order |
| `before` | `string` | No | Tag cursor for pagination. Returns tags that come before this tag in the specified sort order |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of tags to return |
| `name` | `string` | No | Filter tags by name |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for tags. 'asc' for alphabetical order, 'desc' for reverse alphabetical order |
| `order_by` | `name` | No | Field to sort by |
| `query_text` | `string` | No | Filter tags by text search. Deprecated, please use name field instead |



**Output:** `any \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
any | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Templates

### agents.create

`templates.agents.create`

Create Agents From Template. Creates an Agent or multiple Agents from a template. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.templates.agents.create({ template_version: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_version` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_name` | `string` | No | The name of the agent, optional otherwise a random one will be assigned |
| `identity_ids` | `string[]` | No | The identity ids to assign to the agent |
| `initial_message_sequence` | `object[]` | No | Set an initial sequence of messages, if not provided, the agent will start with the default message sequence, if an empty array is provided, the agent will start with no messages |
| `memory_variables` | `Record<string, string>` | No | The memory variables to assign to the agent |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | The tags to assign to the agent |
| `tool_variables` | `Record<string, string>` | No | The tool variables to assign to the agent |

```ts
  role: user | system | assistant,
  batch_item_id?: string,

  name?: string,
  otid?: string,
  sender_id?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The deployment ID for the created agents |
| `group_id` | `string \| null` | No | Optional group ID if agents were created in a group |

### create

`templates.create`

Create template (Cloud-only). Creates a new template from an existing agent or agent file. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.templates.create({
	body: { agent_id: 'example', type: 'agent' },
});
```

| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### delete

`templates.delete`

Delete template (Cloud-only). Deletes all versions of a template with the specified name. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.templates.delete({ template_name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |



---

### rollback

`templates.rollback`

Rollback template to previous version (Cloud-only). Rollback the current working version of a template to a previous saved version. If the current version has unsaved changes, they will be automatically saved as a new version before rollback. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.templates.rollback({ template_name: 'example', version: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `version` | `string` | Yes | The target version to rollback to (e.g., "1", "2", "latest"). Cannot be "current" or "dev". |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `message` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### save

`templates.save`

Save template version (Cloud-only). Saves the current version of the template as a new version. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.templates.save({ template_name: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `block_reconciliation_strategy` | `reconcile-all \| preserve-deleted` | No | Strategy for reconciling memory blocks during migration: "reconcile-all" deletes blocks not in the template, "preserve-deleted" keeps them. Defaults to "preserve-deleted". |
| `message` | `string` | No | A message to describe the changes made in this template version |
| `migrate_agents` | `boolean` | No | If true, existing agents attached to this template will be migrated to the new template version |
| `preserve_core_memories_on_migration` | `boolean` | No | If true, the core memories will be preserved in the template version when migrating agents |
| `preserve_environment_variables_on_migration` | `boolean` | No | If true, the environment variables will be preserved in the template version when migrating agents |
| `preserve_sources_on_migration` | `boolean` | No | If true, existing agent folders/sources will be preserved and merged with template sources during migration. If false, agent sources will be replaced with template sources. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `latest_version` | `string \| null` | No | The latest version of the template |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The exact name of the template |
| `project_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `project_slug` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `template_deployment_slug` | `string \| null` | No | The full name of the template, including version and project slug |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | When the template was last updated |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### update

`templates.update`

Update current template from agent file (Cloud-only). Updates the current working version of a template from an agent file. Letta Cloud answered 404 with no such route for this on 11-27 (free account); it may need a paid plan or have been retired.

| `save_existing_changes` | `boolean` | No | If true, Letta will automatically save any changes as a version before updating the template |
| `update_existing_tools` | `boolean` | No | If true, update existing custom tools source_code and json_schema (source_type cannot be changed) |

### list

`tools.list`

List Tools. Get a list of all tools available to agents.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.tools.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (tool ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `before` | `string` | No | Cursor for pagination (tool ID). Returns results relative to this ID in the specified sort order. Expected format: 'tool-&lt;uuid4&gt;' |
| `exclude_tool_types` | `string[]` | No | Tool type(s) to exclude - accepts repeated params or comma-separated values |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of tools to return |
| `name` | `string` | No | Filter by single tool name |
| `names` | `string[]` | No | Filter by specific tool names |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | Sort order for tools by creation time. 'asc' for oldest first, 'desc' for newest first |
| `order_by` | `created_at` | No | Field to sort by |
| `return_only_letta_tools` | `boolean` | No | Return only tools with tool_type starting with 'letta_' |
| `search` | `string` | No | Search tool names (case-insensitive partial match) |
| `tool_ids` | `string[]` | No | Filter by specific tool IDs - accepts repeated params or comma-separated values |
| `tool_types` | `string[]` | No | Filter by tool type(s) - accepts repeated params or comma-separated values |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts

  id?: string | null,
  args_json_schema?: {
  } | null,

  description?: string | null,
  enable_parallel_execution?: boolean | null,
  json_schema?: {
  } | null,
```

### search

`tools.search`

Search Tools. Search tools using semantic search. Requires tool embedding to be enabled (embed_tools=True). Uses vector search, full-text search, or hybrid mode to find tools matching the query. Returns tools ranked by relevance with their search scores.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.letta.api.tools.search({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `limit` | `number` | No | Maximum number of results to return. |
| `query` | `string` | No | Text query for semantic search. |
| `search_mode` | `vector \| fts \| hybrid` | No | Search mode: vector, fts, or hybrid. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Filter by tags (match any). |
| `tool_types` | `string[]` | No | Filter by tool types (e.g., 'custom', 'letta_core'). |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  combined_score?: number | null,
  tool?: {
    id?: string | null,
    args_json_schema?: {
    } | null,
    created_by_id?: string | null,
    default_requires_approval?: boolean | null,
    description?: string | null,
    enable_parallel_execution?: boolean | null,
    json_schema?: {
    } | null,
    last_updated_by_id?: string | null,
    metadata_?: {
    } | null,
    name?: string | null,
    npm_requirements?: {
      name?: string | null,
      version?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    pip_requirements?: {
      name?: string | null,
      version?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    project_id?: string | null,
    return_char_limit?: number | null,
    source_code?: string | null,
    source_type?: string | null,
    tags?: string[] | null,
    tool_type?: string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | string | null
  } | null,
  embedded_text?: string | null,
  fts_rank?: number | null,
  vector_rank?: number | null
}[]
```
